<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#6E7781;--shiki-dark:#8B949E"> * One SVG that morphs between the four states: a dashed well ring (pending),</span></span>

<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#6E7781;--shiki-dark:#8B949E"> * check (done) and a tinted destructive disc with a drawn cross (failed).</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#6E7781;--shiki-dark:#8B949E"> */</span></span>
